## Aggregated lessons (from recent slices' "Lessons for next slice")

- **A Critic-prompted fix to a parser/matcher is ITSELF a fresh parser claim — run the APED-1 corpus against the FIX, not just the original** (slice-084). The design-Critic's B2 `Z`→`+00:00` fix shipped case-sensitive; only the code-Critic executing lowercase-`z` caught the false-positive STOP. Fuses "a Critic's own fix is a fresh claim" (N+1) with regex/parser-APED-1 / BC-PROJ-13 across personas — the same finding-family propagated design → defective-fix → code-catch. Do NOT collapse the 3-Critic stack.
- **`datetime.fromisoformat` is a three-trap function** (slice-084): tz-naive parses-then-`TypeError`-on-compare; `Z`/`z` case-sensitivity; 3.10-vs-3.11 acceptance split (offset-no-colon / space-separated). Treat external ISO-8601 parsing as a checklist, never a one-liner. **Promoted to BC-GLOBAL-4** (2026-05-30).
- **Undetectable-by-design residuals are legitimate slice outcomes** (slice-084) — narrowing a low/low risk to its detectable sub-case + honestly documenting the open residual (R-23 staler-but-past, undetectable from a single trusted clock) beats forcing a false "retired". Model for partial-mitigation slices; keep the risk `open` (downgraded), don't flip Status.
- **Self-validating-slice property N=5 on the parallel-slice family** (slice-077/078/082/083/084) — well past N=3; "parallel-family slices dogfood their own `/commit-slice --merge`" is a ripe /critic-calibrate codification candidate.

- **New-parser-parity / regex-APED-1 reached N=3 (slice-081/082/083) and was PROMOTED to BC-PROJ-13 (slice-083 /reflect)** — a newly-minted regex/parser's content-shape bug ships through the design-Critic + meta-Critic stack and is caught ONLY by the code-Critic EXECUTING it against an adversarial corpus (slice-081 right-anchored-only matcher; slice-082 no-rstrip heading regex; slice-083 `{7}`-exact + missing diff3 `|||||||` marker). The discipline (an APED-1 adversarial-corpus battery, BOTH false-positive AND false-negative directions, written at design time) is now a build-check, no longer per-slice memory.
- **"A Critic's own fix is a fresh claim" fired N=3 in ONE slice (slice-083)** — meta-Critic M-add-1 (the design-Critic-driven git-native fix reproduced the setext defect), M-add-2 (the other design-fix was unbuildable — named critique agents fail-stop on a missing design.md), AND the code-Critic's M2 fix introduced a docstring SyntaxWarning caught by the suite. Re-interrogate EVERY Builder fix-delta: at the meta-pass, at /code-review, and via the full-suite run. Strongest /critic-calibrate signal to date.
- **3-Critic stack complementarity — strongest single-slice evidence (slice-083, do NOT collapse)** — design-Critic catches design-level (substring-scan setext), meta-Critic catches the Builder's design-fix DELTA (fix reproduced defect / fix was unbuildable), code-Critic catches runtime-execution properties (a regex false-negative on diff3 markers none of the others could reach). Three personas, three non-overlapping defect classes.
- **Self-validating-slice property N=4 on the parallel-slice family** (slice-077/078/082/083) — codification candidate ("parallel-family slices dogfood their own `/commit-slice --merge`").
- **PCR-N taxonomy is now complete** (SOFT auto-regen / VAULT_CLAIM timestamp-winner / HARD+MIXED gate-on-hand-resolve; UNKNOWN fail-closed). The dominant real HARD conflict (`_index.md`-sole) is HIGH-frequency — the lighter-path follow-up `add-index-md-soft-promotion-or-light-hard-path` is the highest-value PCR-N continuation.
- **New-parser-parity discipline now N=2 (slice-081 anchored matcher + slice-082 heading regex)** — when a slice introduces a regex/parser that must agree with an existing canonical parser, write a parity test that feeds the canonical parser's normalization edges (rstrip/strip, CRLF, leading-ws, annotation suffix) BEFORE the design stack signs off. Both instances were caught only by the code-Critic reading the literal; build-check promotion deferred to N=3 (slice-082 /reflect). A /critique-dimension or build-check candidate at N=3. _(Superseded by the N=3 BC-PROJ-13 promotion entry above — slice-083.)_
- **A Critic's own proposed fix is a fresh claim — re-interrogate it at the meta-pass** (slice-078 step-3/4 race + slice-082 M-add-1: the first design-Critic's B1 re-derive-merged_claims fix introduced an unclaimed-candidate over-inclusion that would have broken AC-4 happy-path; only the meta-Critic caught it). The meta-Critic's structural value is reviewing the DELTA the first Critic's fix introduces.
- **Self-validating-slice property N=3 on the parallel-slice family** (slice-077 worktree-awareness / slice-078 PCR-2a / slice-082 equivalence guard) — each touched the parallel-slice family AND is exercisable on its own `/commit-slice --merge`. Codification candidate for PCR-2b ("parallel-family slices dogfood their own merge").
- **Rebase stage assignment is inverted vs ours/theirs intuition** (slice-082): `git rebase <target>` makes stage 2 = rebase-target, stage 3 = replayed-branch (the `_regen_slice_queue` baseline); `_merge_claim_dicts` keeps the stage-2 entry when stage-3's `claimed_at` is absent. PCR test authors: set fixtures by branch + verify the stage→branch mapping empirically.
- **MEPD-1 EXCLUDE for a risk-closing fix-slice with an ADR but no new RULE-ID** (slice-082, extends slice-077/079) — ADR + risk retirement + reflection suffice; no methodology-changelog/VERSION bump, so the AVFS-1/MCFS-1/TVFS-1 forward-sync machinery is a no-op.
- **For any matcher/regex claiming to be "anchored", assert BOTH boundaries in the adversarial test battery** (slice-081) — DCE-1's matcher shipped right-anchored-only despite a "slice-anchored" docstring claim; the code-Critic (reading the literal regex) caught the missing left `\b` that the design + meta Critics both missed. A single "anchored" claim hides two obligations.
- **A new VAULT_ROOT-consuming + version-bumping audit-tool slice touches ~6 second-order drift sites; BC-PROJ-7 enumerates only 2** (slice-081 N=1) — cp1252 list + shippability row are checklisted; the VAULT_ROOT migration allowlist, INSTALL.md count, the per-slice version-sync test rename, and the entry-pin pair are caught late by STP-1 + the full suite. Candidate: extend BC-PROJ-7 to the full fan-out.
- **Procedural "was-an-artifact-produced" gates are a reusable CRP-1-clone family** (CRP-1 critique-review.md → DCE-1 drift-log marker, slice-081) — byte-faithfully clone the CRP-1 skeleton (`_frontmatter_*` / `_resolve_mode` / `_SKIP_VALUE_RE` em-dash / exit-mapping); the meta + code Critics then only need to attack the slice-specific matcher.

- **For bundled-cleanup slices, Phase-B-committed regression tests are the binding contract over design.md prose** — when they disagree, the test wins (code-is-truth); widen the fix to satisfy it + log the delta. (slice-079; design→code gap N=15)
- **The `-x` mid-slice smoke gate mis-fires for all-tests-scaffolded-up-front bundled slices** — read it as "all-built-so-far pass + zero pre-existing regression", not literal exit 0; the un-built phases' WRITTEN-FAILING tests are expected. (slice-079)
- **ACs that mandate editing ANOTHER slice's artifacts must pre-carve-out the archive-immutability path at /slice time** (DEFER-shaped), else they surface as validate-time PARTIALs; cross-slice handoff state wants a LIVE top-level home, not a per-slice-folder file that archives. (slice-079; DEFER-6)
- **When a design row promises a BEHAVIORAL test, realize it** — a structural signature/source-grep pin cannot discriminate the very regression it nominally guards (Fix O / code-review-M1). (slice-079)

- **When a /critique finding spec'es an APED-1-execution discipline FOR ITSELF, run the APED-1 execution against the proposed structural pin literal AT /critique TIME — not at build time.** Slice-078 M3 spec'd Pin #2 in prose; over-broad initial regex (`VAULT_CLAIM[^\n]+fall[- ]closed`) matched legitimate corner-case prose; refinement at build time (`VAULT_CLAIM\s*\([^)]+\)\s*\+\s*HARD\s*\(` — pre-fix multi-class enumeration only). Design→code translation gap N=15 cumulative. **Strongest /critic-calibrate proposal target.** (slice-078)

- **When a Critic finding triggers a Builder-added algorithm spec in the post-fix design, re-interrogate THAT new spec at meta-pass time.** Slice-078 meta-Critic M-add-2 caught the step-3/step-4 disk-read race in the Builder-added 7-step Resolution algorithm: `_pick_loser_replacement` read disk BEFORE write happened; during VAULT_CLAIM rebase-in-progress disk holds conflict markers. Without M-add-2 catch, AC#1 loser-auto-re-pick would silently return `none-available` for every real-world scenario. The Builder's fix surface is fresh code that the original /critique stack didn't review. Pattern N=1 from slice-078; watch for N≥2 codification. (slice-078)

- **MEPD-1 INCLUDE empirically supported when scope refines an existing rule's axis in-place.** Slice-078 PCR-2a refines PCR-1's class taxonomy on existing `PCR-N` family axis (not a new axis) and ships methodology v0.74.0 with 5-part PMI-1 atomic bump correctly. Contrast slice-077 EXCLUDE — both axes (INCLUDE/EXCLUDE) are now empirically grounded and complementary. Inverse counterexample available for future MEPD-1 discrimination guidance. (slice-078)

- **3-Critic stack N=13 cumulative complementarity stable** (slice-063 → slice-078). code-Critic m1-m5 (audit-formatter DRY, PSQ-1 sentinel-overload, atomicity-asymmetry vs PCR-1, design.md stale TF-1 count, sentinel-inheritance documentation) are STRUCTURALLY UNREACHABLE by design-Critic + meta-Critic stack which read mission-brief/design at /critique time but don't run line-level code analysis. Do NOT collapse the 3-Critic stack. (slice-078)

- **Voluntary-restraint discipline N=18 cumulative** (extends slice-077 N=17). Code-Critic v1 advisory findings consistently route to bundled cleanup. `bundle-074-075-077-078-code-critic-cleanup` queue grows; expect slice-079+ to land it. (slice-078)

- **Self-validating-slice property N=2 cumulative** (extends slice-077 N=1) — slice-077 worktree-awareness + slice-078 PCR-2a both touched the parallel-slice family AND structurally dogfooded themselves at /commit-slice --merge time. Watch slice-079 PCR-2b for N=3 trigger toward codification. (slice-078)

- **For ANY structural-pin test that asserts a literal in prose-as-executable-contract surfaces, the pinned literal MUST be UNIQUE-TO-THE-INVOCATION** (not a noun-phrase that appears in informative narration). Slice-075 surfaced this 3× in one slice (bare `git checkout`, bare `git status --porcelain`, bare `2.1.` substring all polluted by adjacent narration). Tighten to invocation-form (`git checkout $default`), line-start anchor (`^2.1.\s`), or wrapping context (`\nLITERAL\n`). APED-1 against pre-fix AND post-fix prose at design time would catch the build-time + post-finish RSAD-1 sub-class. Strongest /critic-calibrate proposal target — extends slice-074's "FIVE simultaneous signals" to **SIX**. (slice-075)

- **For file-move operations within a slice, sweep ALL in-vault references to the old path** — TPHD-1 sub-mode (a) sub-class variant "file-move-but-anchor-not-swept" surfaced slice-075's m2 (5 stale `enable-parallel-slice-pending-items.txt` anchors in mission-brief + design after the file moved to slice scaffold). Cheap defense at file-move time: `grep -r "<old-name>" architecture/slices/<this-slice>/` before commit. TPHD-1 extends to N=8 cumulative with sub-class variant flag (vs prior "edit-but-mirror-not-swept" class). (slice-075)

- **`/critic-calibrate` is now SEVERELY OVERDUE at SIX simultaneous signals** (extends slice-074's FIVE): (1) TPHD-1 sub-mode (a) Builder-fix-block-introduces-N+1-regressions N=8 cumulative with slice-075 sub-class variant; (2) BC-1 BC-GLOBAL-2 prose-vs-automation N=7 cumulative (slice-069/070/071/072/073/074/075); (3) AC-count > 5 N=3 (slice-067/072/074 confirmed; slice-075 deliberately reverted to 5 ACs); (4) MEPD-1-EXCLUDE-vs-shippability-row N=2 (slice-068 + slice-075 confirms axes independent); (5) Scope-expansion-at-/build-slice-plan-mode N=1 (slice-074); (6) **NEW**: RSAD-1 annotation-literal-pollution N=3 this-slice-alone (slice-075 build-time #1+#2 + post-finish code-Critic m1). **Strongest slice-076 candidate** alongside the bundled cleanup. (slice-075)

- **3-Critic stack N=11 cumulative complementarity stable** (slice-063 → slice-075 inclusive) — code-Critic m1 (substring-vs-line-start anchor analysis on `2.1.`) + m2 (file-move stale-anchor sweep) are STRUCTURALLY UNREACHABLE by design-Critic + meta-Critic stack which read mission-brief/design at /critique time but don't run APED-1 on post-fix prose at substring-vs-line-start anchor granularity. Pattern remains stable across 11 cycles. Do NOT collapse the 3-Critic stack. (slice-075)

- **Voluntary-restraint discipline N=16 cumulative** (slice-037/046/050/052/055/056/057/061/065/067/070/071/072/073/074/075) — code-Critic v1 advisory findings consistently route to next-slice bundled cleanup. Pattern structurally stable across 16 cycles. (slice-075)

- **MEPD-1 EXCLUDE + DID add shippability row N=2 cumulative confirmation** (slice-068 + slice-075) — the two axes are independent: methodology-changelog entry/PMI-1 bump is one obligation surface; shippability row is a separate obligation universal to every slice. /critic-calibrate proposal target P4.4: disentangle the two axes in /reflect Step 5.3 prose.
