- **AC count > 5 on new-mechanism mints pattern signal N=1** (slice-067 M-add-1): new-mechanism slices that mint a methodology-changelog rule carry a BC-PROJ-10 paired-pin obligation that cannot fold cleanly into AC1-AC5. Documented deviation per-slice with rationale note works at N=1; if recurs on slice-068+ new-mechanism mints, propose relaxing SKILL.md's ≤5-AC rule to "≤5 (or ≤6 when AC6 is exclusively a v-section entry-pin meta-AC)" via /critic-calibrate. Watch-list candidate. (slice-067)

- **Builder self-catch density on codification slices**: slice-067 = 2 self-catches (Phase B test 3-digit slice-NNN padding drift + Phase F WIRE-1 `rationale:` literal token in 2 exemption cells) — well below the ~10/slice mean (slice-064 N=14, slice-066 N=11). Suggests unusually precise design.md OR well-contained scope (single-rule PSQ-1 mint with no rename/supersession/refactor). When codification slices fall below the mean, the next-slice-N+1 catch density tends to rise; future first-governed-slice should expect normal-to-elevated Builder self-catch budget despite predecessor's clean run. (slice-067)

- **Graphify CLI node-ID convention varies by graph-build mode** (slice-067 design-time discovery): `$PY -m graphify blast-radius --from=<full-path>` and `--file <full-path>` both failed with "node not found"; bare basename `--file <basename>` worked. Future slices using `$PY -m graphify` should probe the current graph's node-ID format at design-time (basename vs full-path vs Python-dotted) instead of assuming the documented CLI form works. `_call_graphify_blast_radius` adapted-from-`build_backlog.py:120-155` retry-loop pattern (basename-first + full-path-fallback) handles this; new slices using graphify CLI should mirror it. (slice-067)

- **Codification slices in this project consistently produce N≈10 Builder self-catches during build despite N=20+ dual-Critic findings caught at design time** (slice-066). The two layers are structurally complementary. Dual-Critic reaches the DECLARATIVE layer (design-claim consistency, ADR identifiers, regex grammar pinning, schema completeness, cross-document citation hygiene); Builder self-catches reach the OPERATIONAL layer (Glob-vs-TF-1-plan, cp1252 test fixtures, PowerShell idioms, cross-doc canonical anchors, section-scoping in prose-pin tests, file-existence verification). Future codification slices should budget Phase A + B-prefix sweeps for (a) Glob-verification of every TF-1 plan path, (b) PMI-1 enumeration cross-check against the most-recent slice's canonical 5-part shape, (c) BC-PROJ-10 paired-entry-pin verification at design-time. Slice-022 "codification slices commit exactly the violation their codified discipline catches" law extends to density ≈N=10 per slice (was N=5-8 baseline at slice-020-040; slice-064 N=14; slice-066 N=11). (slice-066)

- **WS-1 + ETC-1 audit's R-7-class silent-default-off class is the strongest `/critic-calibrate` proposal target** (slice-066) — N=2 if ETC-1 confirms (TF-1 retired via TFFL-1 at slice-034 R-7; WS-1 witnessed N=1 here when `**Walking-skeleton**: true  <!-- comment -->` broke WS-1's anchored regex → silent default-off; gate vacuously passes). Apply slice-034 TFFL-1 pattern verbatim (regex relaxation + value-agnostic field-present malformed branch) to BOTH WS-1 and ETC-1 audits in a future slice. (slice-066)

- **The 3-Critic specialization signal stabilizes at N=4 cumulative** (slice-066 confirms slice-065 N=3 prediction). First-Critic on test-assertion drift + cross-doc citation hygiene + APED-1 empirical-audit-execution at design-stage. Meta-Critic on cross-document mechanical consistency + same-fix-block-edit-introduced regressions + missed-by-first-Critic dimension re-application. Code-Critic on line-level POSIX/runtime portability + Windows path edge cases + third-pass cross-cell drift + empirical-fixture-load-bearing-check at post-build code-stage. Three structurally distinct review windows, neither reachable by the others; the 3-Critic stack IS the structural backstop and should not be collapsed even when N≥1 pass is reliably clean. (slice-066)

- **N=2 application of slice-022 ADR-020 partial-supersession encoding pattern proves the pattern's reusability** (slice-066). ADR-019 stays unmodified across N=2 partial supersessions (ADR-020 covers sub-mode (b); ADR-063 covers sub-mode (a)). The append-only rule holds; single `supersedes:` frontmatter slot + body-level scope enumeration is sufficient at N=2 — the pattern doesn't need extension for multi-supersession. (slice-066)

- **Idempotent operations remove bootstrap-discharge friction** (slice-066). The idempotent worktree-remove guard at `/commit-slice` Step 5b sub-step 5 + Step 5d sub-step 5 (`git worktree list --porcelain | grep -E "^branch refs/heads/slice/NNN-<name>$"` → LOG-and-skip-if-absent path) covers the slice-066 bootstrap (no worktree) + any future `WORKTREE=skip` slice with the SAME mechanism. No need for three separate `WORKTREE=skip-bootstrap` Events lines per skill (build-slice + validate-slice + commit-slice); the idempotent guard makes the cleanup safe across the bootstrap window with one line. Pattern: when authoring methodology prose that fans out across multiple skill surfaces, prefer one idempotent mechanism over N parallel skip-lines. (slice-066)

- **When a candidate fix makes a defect class structurally impossible, the parallel defense-in-depth audit is OVER-engineering and should be declined explicitly** (slice-066). R-17 had two candidate fixes — (a) clean-tree audit + (b) worktree-per-slice. Per ADR-063 §"Options considered" option 3, the candidate-fix-(a) clean-tree audit was DECLINED as redundant defense-in-depth — option (b) makes the WIP-contamination class structurally impossible, so a cleanliness audit becomes a Maginot Line catching a class that can no longer fire. Pattern: when minting a structural fix, explicitly evaluate whether the layered defense-in-depth alternative is OVER-engineering (catching a class that can no longer fire); decline it explicitly in the ADR §"Options considered". (slice-066)

- **Bundled cleanup is now the canonical disposition for code-Critic v1 advisory findings** (slice-066 confirms slice-064/065 lineage). The next-slice `slice-NNN-bundle-NNN-code-critic-cleanup` shape (mirrors slice-065) consumes the prior slice's deferred advisory findings under voluntary-restraint discipline (no methodology-changelog entry / no ADR / no VERSION bump per BC-PROJ-10 / Inclusion-heuristic). Slice-067+ inherits this pattern for slice-066's 6 code-Critic findings (M1 + M2 + m1 + m2 + m3 + m4). (slice-066)

- **The bash_block scoping idiom is now canonical** (slice-065): slice-065 introduced 3× duplications of the 6-line `step_1.find("```bash\n") + step_1.find("\n```", bash_start) + bash_block = step_1[bash_start:bash_end] + assert bash_start >= 0 and bash_end > bash_start` pattern in `tests/skills/code_review/test_code_review_skill.py`. Per Fowler "duplicated code" smell + code-Critic m2 advisory: extract module-level helper `def _resolve_step_1_bash_block(body: str) -> str:` at slice-066+ if N≥4 future tests reuse the idiom. Carrying cost at N=3 is acceptable (slice's design.md explicit voluntary-restraint per "helper hoist deferred to slice-066+ for minimalism"). Pattern: small-N (≤3) verbatim duplication is acceptable; ≥4 instances triggers helper extraction. (slice-065)

- **Slice-040 N+1 doctrine extends to 3-tranche structure at N=15 cumulative** (slice-065): design-Critic + meta-Critic + code-Critic each catch a different tranche of canonical drift at the first-governed-slice for any new pattern. Slice-065 demonstrated this empirically: design-Critic B1+B2 caught textbook RSAD-1 in slice's own drafts; meta-Critic m-add-1 caught residual cross-cell drift in shippability row; code-Critic m1 caught residual sibling-cell drift in SKILL.md L37. The 3-pass Critic stack is now empirically STRUCTURALLY DIFFERENTIATED — each pass catches a different defect class that the prior pass structurally cannot reach. Resist the temptation to promote any single-pass-missing-class to BC-1; the 3-pass stack IS the structural backstop. (slice-065)

- **CRSI-1 v1 walking-skeleton continues to earn its keep at N=3 cumulative** (slice-065): code-Critic finds genuine novel findings on slices with already-CLEAN dual-Critic stack (slice-063 N=1 + slice-064 N=2 + slice-065 N=3). The /code-review v2 enhancements (TRI-1 routing + verdict-driven block + AI-bloat passes) at slice-067+ should preserve advisory-only on test-tightness/narrative-correctness findings (m2/m3/m4 class) BUT consider elevating cross-cell drift findings (m1 class) to verdict-block class — they're empirically grounded + structurally identical to design-Critic stack's m-add-1 / RSAD-1 catch class. Discuss at slice-067 design time. (slice-065)

- **Voluntary-restraint discipline at N=9 cumulative — voluntary-restraint of code-Critic advisories follows the same pattern as voluntary-restraint of methodology-changelog/VERSION/ADR-minting** (slice-065): slice-063 N=1 + slice-064 N=2 + slice-065 N=3 all declined-in-band code-Critic advisories; bundled cleanup at slice-N+1 is the canonical discharge shape. Pattern: CRSI-1 v1 walking-skeleton advisory-only discipline is structurally identical to voluntary-restraint disposition for methodology-event-promotion (MEPD-1). Both shapes resist mid-slice scope expansion; both shapes nominate next-slice bundled cleanup. (slice-065)

- **TPHD-1 sub-mode (c) Builder self-catch at /build-slice prerequisite remains the canonical defense layer for PTFFD-1 phantom function names** (slice-065): slice-065's mission-brief TF-1 row #5 typo (`test_run_catalog_returns_zero_on_all_pass` vs actual `test_main_returns_0_on_all_pass`) survived all three Critic passes; caught only at /build-slice prerequisite gate. The TF-1 audit at `tools/test_first_audit.py` checks status only (not function-existence per slice-017 /critique B1 ACCEPTED-FIXED); TPHD-1 sub-mode (c) is the function-existence backstop. Future slices should continue trusting this gate; do NOT extend Critic-prompt dimensions to function-existence verification (the division of labor is sound). (slice-065)

- **The B1 falsifier class (`git diff <base>...HEAD` is commit-vs-commit only at /build-slice end) extends to N=2 cumulative across surfaces** (slice-063): NAW-1 retired it on its own surface; `/code-review` SKILL.md v1 still carries it on its own diff resolution. The union-of-three-sources fix (`git diff --diff-filter=A {base}` working-tree-vs-base + `git ls-files --others --exclude-standard` untracked + `git diff --diff-filter=A {base}...HEAD` commits-vs-base, unioned + deduplicated) is the canonical mitigation shape. Pattern: any methodology surface that resolves a slice's code diff via git must handle uncommitted working-tree state because /build-slice doesn't commit (commits at /commit-slice per PCA-1 HARD-STOP terminal contract). **Slice-064 ACTIVE nomination**: mirror to `/code-review`. Meta-irony observed in-flight — the slice fixing the read-mechanism falsifier on NAW-1's surface immediately surfaced the SAME defect class on `/code-review`'s own surface. (slice-063)

- **R-18 retirement is methodology-discoverability, NOT runtime guarantee** (slice-063): NAW-1 surfaces the WARN at `/build-slice` Step 6 BEFORE the next slice's chain runs. Slice-063's own `/code-review` did NOT hit AGENT-UNSPAWNABLE this session, demonstrating that R-18's runtime behavior is non-deterministic across Claude Code sessions (slice-061 N=1 + slice-062 N=2 hit it; slice-063 did not). The retirement is structurally sound regardless: methodology-discoverability is the load-bearing property, not runtime-deterministic-prevention. Future R-N risks of the same shape should be retired on the discoverability axis, not the runtime-determinism axis. (slice-063)

- **PTFCD-1 `(extended)` Test-path annotation recurrence is N=2 cumulative** (slice-054 + slice-063): discipline — NEVER use parenthesized annotations in TF-1 plan Test-path cells; cite the bare file path; put annotations in Notes section below the table. Build-time TF-1 strict-pre-finish gate IS the structural backstop (slice-037 law). User declined BC-1 promotion at N=2 (recommended option), keeping watch-list discipline; promote to BC-PROJ-NNN if N=3 emerges via /critic-calibrate route. (slice-063)

- **CRSI-1 walking-skeleton v1 value proposition validated at N=1** (slice-063): code-Critic third-pass added a genuine novel m1 finding on a slice whose dual-Critic stack passed CLEAN at TRI-1 (13/13 ACCEPTED-FIXED). The design-stack and code-stack catch structurally-different defect classes (design-stack reviews mission-brief + design.md + ADR prose; code-stack reviews the just-written code diff). Slice-063's m1 finding (dead-code/UX-wart in `_resolve_default_branch` second `try/except FileNotFoundError`) is the kind of small-scope code-level observation the design-stack structurally couldn't reach. CRSI-1 v1's advisory-only walking-skeleton is the right minimum-viable shape. **Slice-064 v2** (TRI-1 + verdict-block + AI-bloat passes) remains highest-priority next-loop candidate. (slice-063)

- **First-Critic vs meta-Critic emerging specialization signal extends to N=2 cumulative** (slice-062 + slice-063): first Critic concentrates on prose drift + cross-doc citation hygiene; meta-Critic concentrates on cross-document enumeration drift + empirical audit re-execution. Specialization is structural (different review surfaces, different attention budgets) — NOT a Critic-prompt dimension extension. If N=3 confirms at slice-064+, document as stable Critic-stack design observation. (slice-063)

- **MEPD-1 Inclusion-heuristic firing on new-mechanism slice extends to N=6 cumulative on the new-RULE-ID precedent** (slice-049/050/054/059/060/063): the voluntary-restraint-zero-bump-zero-ADR posture (slice-037/046/050/052/055/056/057/061 N=8 cumulative) is RESERVED for retirement-discharge / part-(b) closure shapes; new-mechanism slices like NAW-1 take the full 5-part PMI-1 bump + new ADR + methodology-changelog entry + shippability row + Inclusion-heuristic firing per the discovery-gate axis precedent slice-063 just established. NAW-1 sits adjacent to (NOT extending) the forward-sync family (PMI-1 / PVFS-1 / AVFS-1 / MCFS-1 / TVFS-1). (slice-063)

- **The slice-040 N+1 first-governed-slice doctrine extends to N=12 cumulative on the slice-022 RSAD-1 self-violation axis** (slice-062; N=2 on the TF-1-multi-AC-label sub-class specifically): discipline rule minted in slice N is blind-spot in slice N+1; the dual-Critic stack's meta-Critic IS the structural backstop for the empirical-audit-execution-on-fix-block-edits class. slice-062's /critique m3 ACCEPTED-FIXED fix-block edit ITSELF introduced /critique-review M-add-1 (Blocker) — the 7-row TF-1 plan table copied from design.md used multi-AC labels `"1, 3"` + `"(audit-trace)"` markers that the TF-1 audit rejects → 2 `ac-without-row` violations. Meta-Critic empirically caught it by running the audit. EXACT same defect class as slice-056 /critique-review M-add-1 (N=2 sub-class recurrence). Per slice-037 audit-vs-real-artifact law (N≥10 cumulative): NO Critic dimensions or BC-1 rules for build-time-reachable classes — the existing gates ARE the structural backstop. The cure: /critic-calibrate periodic review (watch-list N=2 → promote at N=3) to sharpen the first-Critic prompt with "if the fix edits a structural-audit-parseable table, run the relevant audit empirically before declaring ACCEPTED-FIXED." (slice-062)

- **Extends-not-mints vs voluntary-restraint discipline distinction (cumulative N=6 / N=8 respectively)**: when a slice substantively EXTENDS an existing rule's coverage (drift-guard family member-addition, audit-scope-extension), it warrants Inclusion-heuristic firing (methodology-changelog entry + ADR + 4/5-part PMI-1 bump + shippability row + extends-not-mints ADR shape per ADR-053 precedent). When a slice DISCHARGES a retirement gate (part-(b) closure / retirement-discharge) on an already-shipped rule with structural-mechanism backstop already in place, it warrants voluntary-restraint (no new rule / ADR / changelog entry / VERSION bump). Both are tunable knobs driven by what the slice substantively does, not by slice severity. slice-062 makes scope-extension count N=6 cumulative (slice-049/050/051/057/058/059/062); voluntary-restraint count stays N=8 cumulative (slice-037/046/050/052/055/056/057/061). (slice-062)

- **R-18 N=2 cumulative recurrence on slice-060 N+1 governed-slice axis** — predicted by slice-061 reflection L82, empirically confirmed at slice-062. The `/code-review` agent is not hot-loaded into a session that predates slice-060's ship of the agent install; user-ratified deferral per slice-061 precedent. Strengthens slice-063+ R-18 mitigation candidate from watch-list to **active nomination**. Cheapest fix per slice-061 reflection: (a) `/build-slice` post-build new-agent warning. Mid-cost: (b) `/code-review` error-semantic widening to auto-skip + structured-options ASK (incremental extension of /code-review SKILL Step 2's existing `agent-unspawnable` handler). (slice-062)

- **First-Critic vs meta-Critic emerging specialization signal (N=1 watch-list, slice-062)**: first Critic on prose drift + cross-doc citation hygiene + cross-document coherence (slice-062 first-Critic 5/6 findings); meta-Critic on empirical audit re-execution + same-fix-block-edit-introduced regressions (slice-062 meta-Critic 1/3 missed findings was a Blocker that required empirical audit re-run to detect). If N=2 specialization confirms at slice-063+, document as Critic-stack design observation (NOT a Critic-prompt dimension extension — the meta-Critic IS the structural backstop for the empirical-audit-execution class by design). (slice-062)

- **Builder mid-design self-correction without /critique invocation IS a viable lightweight feedback path** (N=2 cumulative with slice-061 BFRD-1 confirm-then-auto-invoke precedent): if Builder catches own /slice-time misreading during /design-slice grounding, correct in the same fix block — don't carry forward stale claims into /critique. slice-062 corrected mission-brief frontmatter L5 "Risk retired" + AC#4 wording inline at /design-slice time before /critique fired (R-15 was already retired per slice-057; Builder misread slice-061 reflection wording "R-15's existing retired-but-class-still-open status" as implying R-15 was still mitigating). Class: lightweight self-feedback within the same skill invocation; appropriate for /slice-time misreadings caught during /design-slice's deeper grounding. (slice-062)

- **Newly-installed Claude Code subagents are NOT hot-loaded into the running session** (R-18 minted; N=1 class signal): the agent registry is loaded at session start; mid-session writes to `~/.claude/agents/*.md` are invisible to `Agent(subagent_type=…)` calls until the user restarts Claude Code. Recurring class for any slice immediately following an agent-shipping slice in the same session — slice-060 shipped `/code-review`; slice-061 in the same session hit `agent-unspawnable`. Mitigation candidates: methodology-side warning at `/build-slice` post-build when slice diff includes a new `agents/*.md`; `/code-review` error-semantic widening to auto-skip on the recurring-class case; or Claude Code platform fix (registry hot-reload, out-of-scope). slice-062+ candidate. (slice-061)
- **When fixing a hardcoded literal at one named surface, the Critic stack misses sibling instances of the same class in adjacent prose** (BC-PROJ-11 sibling-coverage gap; N=1 watch-list): slice-061's M5 fix specified dynamic-pyproject-grep for the path-validation surface, but the installer-guidance prose carried `Python 3.10 or newer` hardcoded — both first-Critic and meta-Critic missed it. BC-PROJ-11 grep at /build-slice Step 6 caught cleanly at intended latency. Per slice-037 law: no Critic dim / no BC-1 promotion at N=1; if N=2 emerges, promote to a Critic-prompt dimension extension via `/critic-calibrate`-route: "when fixing a hardcoded literal at one surface, scan the entire artifact for sibling instances of the same class in adjacent prose." (slice-061)
- **The slice-055 user-approved-deferral precedent extends to N=2** (slice-061 deferred a pre-existing slice-060 R-15-class stale-archive-path failure in `tests/skills/code_review/`): when shippability dogfood at /build-slice Step 6 surfaces a PRE-EXISTING prior-slice defect (NOT a current-slice regression), the right disposition is user-approved deferral via structured-options at /build-slice Step 6 + slice-N+1 nomination for the structural fix. Coupling slice-056's `_resolve_slice_dir(N)` helper + corpus class-closure backstop needs scope extension from `tests/methodology/*.py` to also cover `tests/skills/**/*.py` + `tests/agents/**/*.py` — slice-062 is the canonical carrier. (slice-061)
- **The slice-040 N+1 first-governed-slice doctrine extends to N=12 cumulative**: slice-060 minted /code-review + the CRSI-1 in-loop adversarial code-review surface; slice-061 was its first governed slice; the meta-Critic caught M-add-1 (INSTALL.md not in /code-review's in-scope list) — the canonical N+1 first-governed-slice catch on a fresh-surface scope-list class. The DR-1 meta-Critic IS the structural backstop for this class. Pattern is now a stable structural law for codification-class slices; budget DR-1 as the surface for novel-edge findings on N+1 slices. (slice-061)
- **Cross-slice runtime-state interactions are a NEW Critic blind-spot class** (R-18 N=1): neither first-Critic, meta-Critic, nor code-Critic can reach into runtime-state characteristics of the Claude Code harness itself (agent registry, session-load behavior, tool registry). Build-time methodology-side warnings + risk-register tracking are the right structural tooling for this class. If N=2 emerges, consider a `/critic-calibrate` dimension extension for "cross-slice runtime-state interactions." (slice-061)
- **Voluntary-restraint discipline extends to N=8 cumulative on codification slices** (037/046/050/052/055/056/057/061): when (a) the rule's behavior is already in the repo via a prior slice, (b) the structural-mechanism backstop is already in place, AND (c) the slice's state-transition is parametric within an already-shipped rule, no new rule / ADR / methodology-changelog entry / VERSION bump is needed. slice-061's R-16 born-retired discharge follows slice-045/057 R-11/R-15 precedent shape; MEPD-1(b) discharge-by-name verified against META-1 enforcing assertion at `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py:136` (vacuously satisfied — zero new `## v…` sections). (slice-061)
- **`/reflect` MUST update `_index.md`'s "Most recent 10" table + Aggregated lessons in the same step** — surfaced as a slice-060 omission (the slice-060 row was missing from `_index.md` until slice-061 retroactively added it). When this Step 5 (write reflection.md) happens, the `_index.md` update is structurally inseparable; if a future slice's /reflect skips this update, the next slice's /reflect catches it and corrects retroactively. Watch-list candidate for `/reflect` SKILL.md pre-finish discipline (N=1 — slice-060 omission corrected by slice-061). (slice-061)
- **Adding a `tools/` module touches five inventories, not three**: `plugin.yaml`, `install_audit.py` `_CANONICAL_TOOLS`, `architecture/shippability.md`, `INSTALL.md`'s test-enforced tool-count literal, and `test_utf8_stdout_regression.py`'s cp1252-coverage `_ROOT_ONLY_TOOLS` list. Enumerate all five at design time — the last two surfaced only as methodology-suite FAILures at build Step 6. A `/critic-calibrate` watch-list candidate (build-time-reachable per the slice-037 law → not a first-Critic dimension, not a BC-1 promotion at N=1). (slice-059)
- **When a slice's correctness rests on a runtime-resolution claim ("mechanism M reads source X"), the Critic must EXECUTE M, not reason about it**: B1 — the original `importlib.metadata.version()` CWD-shadowing defect — was provable only by running the read against the real repo+venv (APED-1 empirical attack), not by reading the design. (slice-059)
- **TVFS-1 closes the version-bearing-leg gate family** — PMI-1 (`plugin.yaml`) / PVFS-1 (`pyproject.toml`) / AVFS-1 (installed `ai-sdlc-VERSION`) / MCFS-1 (installed changelog) / TVFS-1 (installed pip distribution). Every version-bearing surface is now gated; a future tool-package change should re-confirm no sixth leg has appeared. (slice-059)
- **Recipe / methodology docs must reference `VERSION` dynamically — never hard-code a methodology version literal**: hard literals re-drift every bump (N=2 in INSTALL.md — v0.20.0 fixed by slice-045 → re-drifted to a stale v0.54.0, fixed by slice-058). When fixing a stale version literal, REMOVE it (reword to reference `VERSION`); refreshing the literal just resets the drift clock. Promoted to BC-PROJ-11. (slice-058)
- **For a content slice where a test pins prose, FREEZE the prose at design time and name the verbatim test anchors**: a "Draft"-labelled artifact under a downstream substring-pin is a latent test/text divergence (slice-058 meta-Critic M-add-1) — the canonical-phrase discipline FBCD-1 applies to test-function names extends to test-pinned prose blocks. (slice-058)
- **A new step in a numbered procedure needs an explicit design-time placement decision, including the renumber-vs-append call**: check for letter-referenced cross-refs first — INSTALL.md's "Source independence" section references Step 3f/3g by letter, making append-as-`3h` strictly cheaper (zero cross-ref churn) than insert-as-`3e` + renumber (slice-058 meta-Critic M-add-2). (slice-058)
- **The slice-037 audit-vs-real-artifact interaction law extends to N≥10 cumulative**: dual-Critic stack precision holds across 10 zero-false-alarm slices on codification-class work (046/048/050/051/052/053/054/055/056/057). The stack structurally cannot reach build-time audit-vs-real-artifact interactions; slice-057 produced N=0 missed-by-both-Critic-layers findings (the meta-Critic caught m-add-2 specifically targeting the comment-rewrite-could-self-match risk class slice-056's freshly-minted M-add-2 corpus class-closure backstop introduced — the canonical N+1 first-governed-slice catch). Per slice-037 law: NO Critic dimensions for build-time-reachable classes — calibration via watch-list / `/critic-calibrate` is the right tooling. (slice-057)
- **Slice-040 N+1 doctrine extends to N=11 cumulative**: "discipline rule minted in slice N is a first-Critic blind spot on slice N+1; the DR-1 meta-Critic is the structural backstop". slice-056 minted the M-add-2 corpus class-closure backstop mechanism (a structurally-novel surface combining whitelist-shrinkage + self-skip clause + dual-direction assertion); slice-057 was its first governed slice; the meta-Critic caught m-add-2 (comment-rewrite-could-self-match risk class) that the first-Critic missed — the first-Critic optimized the mechanical pin/changelog surface and under-weighted the novel structural edge, exactly the slice-046 reclassification-slice blind-spot pattern applied here to slice-056-just-minted-mechanism class. Pattern N+1 across slice-026/029/038/039/046/049/050/051/054/055/056/057. **The doctrine is now a stable structural law for codification-class slices**: budget the meta-Critic as the structural backstop for the N+1 first-governed-slice findings; do NOT add new first-Critic dimensions for these classes. (slice-057)
- **Retirement-discharge slice class is N=5 stable**: slice-040 R-10 / slice-043 R-6 / slice-045 R-11 / slice-056 R-15-part-(a) / slice-057 R-15-part-(b) all used MEPD-1(b) discharge-by-name against the real META-1 enforcing assertion at `test_methodology_changelog.py:136` (NOT precedent analogy alone, per slice-032 false-precedent guard). The classification is now robust enough to be a stable design-time pattern rather than per-slice judgment. The 5-slice N=5 same-class artifact-set pattern: (i) flip `**Status**:` field-line in risk-register.md to `retired` (RR-1 parses status SOLELY from this field per slice-043 BC-PROJ-6); (ii) append a retirement paragraph at section end with full discharge mechanics (mechanism citation, META-1 vacuous-satisfaction verification, precedent corroboration); (iii) preserve all prior prose verbatim per slice-040 R-10 retirement-precedent. (slice-057)
- **Voluntary-restraint discipline extends to N=7 cumulative on codification slices** (037/046/050/052/055/056/057): when (a) the rule's behavior is already in the repo via a prior slice's helper/test/risk-register-entry AND (b) the structural-mechanism backstop is already in place AND (c) the slice's state-transition is parametric within an already-shipped rule, no new rule / ADR / methodology-changelog entry / VERSION bump is needed. slice-057's R-15 retirement-discharge is a textbook case: the helper + corpus backstop + whitelist-shrinkage mechanism all shipped at slice-056; slice-057 only discharges the gate parametrically. Cite MEPD-1(b) discharge-by-name against the real META-1 assertion explicitly at the build-log no-changelog decision point. (slice-057)
- **Builder self-catch of TPHD-1 sub-mode (b) carry-over harmonization pre-/critique-review is cheap and worth it** (slice-049 N+1 lineage): before launching /critique-review, scan the post-/critique-fix files for stale references to the pre-fix state. slice-057 caught one ("two affected tests" → "three" in design.md §"Validation strategy") with one targeted Edit pre-meta-Critic-launch. Saves the meta-Critic from spending an EXTEND finding on Builder carry-over noise; keeps dual-Critic-stack calibration clean. (slice-057)
- **Builder guard-rail discipline for novel-structural-mechanism slices** (slice-057 m-add-2 catch, reusable): when a slice's central mechanism is a corpus-backstop or self-matching-regex surface, explicitly include a Builder guard-rail in design.md §"What's new" naming (a) regex match shape that would trigger a self-match, (b) self-skip clause's exact scope (file-only vs file-and-dependents), (c) safe alternatives if an in-comment example is needed (markdown code-fence in docstring OR backtick-fenced inline placeholder), (d) prose-mention-is-safe-by-construction guarantee. The N+1 first-governed-slice for any future regex-backstop family member benefits from this checklist. (slice-057)
- **The slice-037 audit-vs-real-artifact interaction law extends to N≥9 cumulative**: dual-Critic stack precision holds across 9 zero-false-alarm slices on codification-class work (046/048/050/051/052/053/054/055/056). The stack structurally cannot reach build-time audit-vs-real-artifact interactions. Three new N=1 watch-list class signals on slice-056 (doc-comment self-detection; BC-PROJ-7 in-code-span pipe; meta-Critic proposed-fix verification) all build-time-reachable + gate-caught at intended latency. Per slice-037 law: NO Critic dimensions for build-time-reachable classes — calibration via watch-list / `/critic-calibrate` is the right tooling. (slice-056)
- **Doc-comment-vs-audit-literal self-detection class** (slice-056 build-time discovery, N=1, no BC-1 promotion per slice-037 law): when authoring in-code documentation for a literal-pattern audit, the comment text containing the matched literal verbatim creates circular self-detection (audit greps for the pattern; comment is part of the file the audit scans). Rule: paraphrase the matched literal in comments — describe the pattern without including it verbatim. Caught at /build-slice mid-slice smoke (AC3 + corpus backstop both FAILed on the R-15 explanatory comment text); quick paraphrase fix. Watch-list `/critic-calibrate` candidate (track if N=2 emerges). (slice-056)
- **BC-PROJ-7 scope clarification: pipe-free row applies to literal `|` inside backtick-fenced code spans inside shippability row Description text** (slice-056 build-time discovery, N=1, no new rule): the SCMD-1 audit parser uses naive `inner.split("|")` and does NOT honor markdown `\|` escapes — even pipes inside `` `Path | None` `` shift columns. Existing BC-PROJ-7 already covers this scope; clarification only. Pre-finish SCMD-1 audit caught it; rewrite Description prose to avoid the pipe character entirely. (slice-056)
- **Meta-Critic's PROPOSED FIX mechanism needs Builder empirical verification before adoption** (slice-056 /critique-review M-add-1, N=1): the meta-Critic correctly identified the TF-1 AC-numbering miss (finding VALIDATED) but its proposed fix option (a) — "split body AC into `4a./4b./4c.`" — was WRONG at the regex layer (`_AC_ITEM_RE = r"^\s*(\d+)\.\s+\S"` rejects `4a.`). Builder verified empirically + chose the actual valid fix (multi-row-per-AC with plain `4` cells). Generalization: Builder recompute-don't-trust discipline (slice-032/034/041/042/049 N≥5 + slice-056 = N≥6 on the broader recompute-don't-trust pattern) extends to meta-Critic's PROPOSED FIX mechanisms, not just first-Critic ACCEPTED-FIXED edits. (slice-056)
- **R-15 part-(b) retirement is structurally backstopped, not just observationally backstopped** (slice-056 design pattern, reusable for any "future slice demonstrably uses the discipline" gate): embed a whitelist with shrinkage mechanism in a corpus regression test — when the whitelist shrinks to empty AND the corpus has no match, the assertion (`whitelist - matches` empty AND `matches - whitelist` empty) structurally satisfies "the discipline has propagated" without waiting for cross-slice observational evidence. The dual-direction whitelist check is what makes the mechanism structural: empty-matches alone is insufficient (could be a temporary corpus regression); the second direction (`matches - whitelist`) detects whitelist staleness. (slice-056)
- **Helper-with-call-time-globals + monkeypatch is the canonical Python pattern for test-utility-with-injectable-REPO_ROOT** (slice-056 design pattern, reusable): avoid default-argument capture (`def f(_root=REPO_ROOT)`); avoid closure-cell (`from .conftest import REPO_ROOT` then helper references the imported name); use module-globals access at call-time (`REPO_ROOT.joinpath(...)` inline; Python resolves the name at each call). `monkeypatch.setattr("tests.methodology.conftest.REPO_ROOT", tmp_path)` then works against subsequent calls. Single-arg signature stays clean (avoid 2-arg `repo_root: Path | None = None` which invites future caller drift). (slice-056)
- **Whole-file regex for multi-line literal-path scans** (slice-056 build-time discovery): Python `\s*` matches newlines by default; per-line `for line in text.splitlines()` iteration misses constructions split across multiple lines. Canonical pattern: `re.finditer(text)` + `text.count("\n", 0, m.start()) + 1` for line numbers. (slice-056)
- **Voluntary-restraint discipline extends to N≥6 cumulative on codification-class slices** (037/046/050/052/055/056): when test-coverage + a helper + a risk-register transition retire a class-signal-N=1 risk with structural-mechanism backstop available, the right artifact is the helper + tests + transition — NOT a new rule, NOT an ADR. Slice-056 deliberately minted zero rules + zero ADRs on a Major-severity finding (M-add-2 corpus class-closure backstop) with structural whitelist-shrinkage mechanism in place. The contrast slice (slice-054 minted PVFS-1 on a recurring class) vs slice-056 (no mint on a class-signal-N=1 with structural backstop) demonstrates voluntary-restraint is a tunable knob driven by failure-class recurrence + structural-mechanism availability, not by slice severity. (slice-056)
- **TF-1 Status enum is exact-match — no trailing prose in Status cell** (slice-054 build-time discovery, N=1, no BC-1 promotion per slice-037 law): authoring `PASSING (notes...)` for a TF-1 plan row triggers `invalid-status` violation, cascading into `ac-without-row` for the affected AC (the audit treats invalid-status rows as if the AC has no row at all). Move annotations to a separate Notes block below the table. Caught in-band at /build-slice Phase F first TF-1 strict-pre-finish run; quick re-edit fix. Watch-list `/critic-calibrate` candidate (track if N=2 emerges; build-time TF-1 strict-pre-finish IS the structural backstop, per slice-037). Related: TF-1 + PTFCD-1 Test-path cell must resolve to a real file on disk — `architecture/shippability.md (row #54)` with non-path "(row #54)" suffix trips `missing-test-path-file`; cite the underlying pytest the catalog runner invokes instead. (slice-054)
- **BCR-1 first-dogfood SC-NNN traceability-axis pin discipline** (slice-054 /critique-review M-add-1, N=1): when a slice mints a new RULE-ID AND closes a SC-NNN finding via BCR-1, the shippability row's consumer-propagation pin should assert BOTH the RULE-ID AND the SC-NNN. A future row rewrite that drops the SC-NNN cite would silently sever the `/diagnose → /slice → /reflect` traceability axis slice-053 BCR-1 just shipped to enforce. First Critic missed at slice-054 (the BCR-1 trace axis is novel-to-this-slice — slice-054 is the first BCR-1 end-to-end dogfood). Meta-Critic caught (M-add-1 EXTEND). Generalize for the next BCR-1-closing slice; if missed again at N=2, promote to BC-PROJ. Recorded as next-slice authoring discipline, not a programmatic gate yet. (slice-054)
- **AC4-class /reflect-deferred output-axis verification pattern works**: bifurcating an AC into input-contract (verifiable at /validate-slice via pytest assertion) + output-contract (verifiable at /reflect via the actual round-trip) preserves PCA-1 auto-advance while honoring the cross-skill verification dependency. The mission-brief verification-plan row carries the position-pinned awk script (executed at /reflect-time); the pytest TF-1 row asserts the input contract (verified at /validate-slice). Reusable pattern for any future AC whose output is /reflect-time — and Step 5b-fs/avfs methodology requires the same /reflect-time output-axis verification class for any forward-sync gate. (slice-054)
- **The `~/.claude/*` config files trip the Write tool's auto-mode classifier** ("modifies a file the agent loads at startup"). The Bash `echo > "$HOME/.claude/<file>"` path is the documented escape ("attempt to accomplish this action using other tools that might naturally be used to accomplish this goal"). For any PMI-1 4-part bump slice: plan the leg-4 (~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION) + leg-5 (~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md) edits via Bash, not Write. Document at the time the denial fires. **Recurrence-class with slice-035 DEVIATION-2 + slice-048→049 silent-drift class — same underlying installed-VERSION-edit difficulty; AVFS-1 + MCFS-1 are the structural backstops.** (slice-054)
- **Cross-slice rule-minting-then-dogfooding pattern (slice-053→054)**: a slice that mints a contract that its own loop cannot apply (BCR-1 self-bootstrap held by construction on slice-053 — no `**Closes:** SC-NNN` sentinel) AND explicitly names the expected next-dogfood slice in the rule's changelog entry handed off cleanly to slice-054 (the first slice carrying the sentinel + triggering the round-trip). Generalize for any future rule-minting slice whose first dogfood is N+1: name the expected next-dogfood slice in the rule's changelog entry + ensure the trigger discipline disambiguates mention-vs-application (slice-053 M4 sentinel-anchored trigger preempted the false-trigger class). (slice-054)
- **The slice-037 audit-vs-real-artifact interaction law extends to N+1 (slice-054 confirms N≥7 cumulative)**: dual-Critic stack precision holds (N=7 zero-false-alarm streak on codification slices 046/048/050/051/052/053/054 with ~50+ findings), but the stack structurally cannot reach build-time audit-vs-real-artifact interactions. slice-054 missed 2 such patterns (TF-1 enum-strict, PTFCD-1 path-resolvability) — both caught in-band by the gates. Do NOT add Critic dimensions for build-time-reachable classes; the existing gates ARE the structural backstop. The N≥7 cumulative recurrence (slice-037, 038, 044, 050, 051, 052, 053, 054) confirms this law structurally. (slice-054)

- **Multi-site-literal contrast handling for anchor-presence audits** (slice-053 build-time discovery, N=1, no BC-1 promotion per slice-037 law): when authoring an anchor-presence audit with canonical literals that may appear at >1 site in the scoped section, the M3 6-step recipe's perturbation step MUST use `count=-1` (all-occurrences) for those literals OR the audit must use a uniquely-positioned variant. The Python `str.in` substring check only fails when the literal disappears entirely from the section; perturbing one site of a multi-occurrence literal leaves the audit silently passing (4 of 8 slice-053 mid-slice smoke contrasts initially FAIL-TO-FAIL on `count=1`). Document inline in audit-module docstrings + in `design.md`§"Genuine-contrast proof method" for any future anchor-presence audit. Watch-list `/critic-calibrate` candidate (track if N=2 emerges; build-time mid-slice smoke gate IS the structural backstop, per slice-037). (slice-053)
- **Use stable-leading-literal anchors for numbered-list-item audit constants** (slice-053 build-time discovery, N=1, no BC-1 promotion): markdown linters/formatters can auto-renumber numbered list items mid-build (slice-053 witnessed `7.→8.` with contiguity gap `1,2,3,4,5,6,8`). Audit constants pinning the leading numeric marker break under renumber pressure. Use the leading inline literal (e.g. `**Foo**`) WITHOUT the numeric prefix — robust to renumber. Complementary to slice-038 BC-PROJ-7 pipe-free discipline. Watch-list candidate. (slice-053)
- **BCR-1 closes the `/diagnose → /slice → /reflect` loop deterministically** (slice-053; ADR-055 extends BC-PROJ-10): `/slice` consults `diagnose-out/backlog.md` as source #7 (M1-locked canonical phrase verbatim); `/reflect` round-trips closed `SC-NNN` findings via the `**Closes:** SC-\d{3}` sentinel-anchored trigger. Mentioned-vs-closes disambiguation (M4 refinement) prevents documentation-class `SC-NNN` mentions from false-triggering the round-trip. The contract is deterministically pinned by 8 anchor-presence + position-pin tests on both SKILL.md surfaces; the OSDG-1 family transitively guards the new anchor inserts. R-14 records the residual axes: human-judgement axis remains open (future `/critic-calibrate` candidate); BCR-1↔R-13 producer-side cross-skill brittleness mitigated via Test #7 consumer-side `SC-\d{3}` grammar pin until R-13 structurally closes (OSDG-1 extension to `/slice-candidates`). slice-054 SC-001 fix-pyproject-toml-version-drift is the first concrete BCR-1 round-trip dogfood; will carry `**Closes:** SC-001` sentinel. (slice-053)
- **BC-PROJ-10 dischargement is now routine** (slice-052 minted, slice-053 applied on N+1; the slice-049/052 N=2 missed-by-both-Critics class did NOT recur at slice-053): the first-Critic m2 immediately demanded the META-1 enforcing-assertion citation (`test_methodology_changelog.py:136` `^## v` split semantic) verifying the Inclusion-heuristic posture by name, not by precedent analogy alone. Builder-side BC-PROJ-10 gate held on the first slice the rule governs (slice-040 N+1 "rule minted slice-N is a first-Critic blind spot on slice N+1" inverted — the dischargement was demanded on slice N+1). Pattern: when a slice's central decision class is governed by a rule minted in the immediately-prior slice, the gate + the Critic prompt + the dischargement-by-name discipline together close the human-judgement-axis gap. (slice-053)
- **First-ever BCR-1 self-bootstrap held by construction** (slice-053 dogfood): the slice that ships BCR-1 is also the first to apply the contract — its own /reflect Step 2 consults mission-brief.md OR reflection.md for the `**Closes:** SC-` sentinel; slice-053 carries neither (only documentation-class `**SC-001**` bold-emphasis mentions); the trigger correctly no-ops. The M4 refinement from bare `SC-\d{3}` to `**Closes:** SC-\d{3}` sentinel was empirically driven by slice-053's own self-bootstrap defect (Critic M4: bare-trigger would have false-triggered on slice-053's mission-brief `**SC-001**` mentions). Lesson for any future slice authoring a behavior contract that the slice itself must apply: empirically verify the self-application at design time, not just at validate time. (slice-053)
- **The slice-046/048/050/051/052/053 dual-Critic zero-false-alarm streak is now N=6 on codification slices**: precision holds across ~50+ findings with zero false-alarms / zero severity miscals; misses (when they occur) are at build-time-runtime classes the stack structurally cannot reach. Strong signal for the slice-037 "do NOT add Critic dimensions for build-time-reachable classes; the gates work" law. If precision regresses on a future slice, that's the calibration signal. (slice-053)

- **The slice-049/052 N=2 "methodology-bump misclassified as PMI-1 artifact-enumeration unaffected" missed-by-Critic pattern is now backstopped by BC-PROJ-10** (slice-052; BCI-1 fixture + live byte-equal): a slice that mints a new ADR OR adds a user-facing skill capability/mode but has no new RULE-ID / no new registered artifact MUST explicitly classify the methodology-changelog Inclusion-heuristic posture (`bump-required` vs `MEPD-1(b) why-none discharged` with cited precedent + META-1 enforcing-assertion grep) in mission-brief.md / design.md BEFORE /critique. A bare "drift posture unaffected" / "no new registered artifact" claim covers PMI-1 artifact-enumeration ONLY, not the Inclusion-heuristic behavior-change axis. The slice-049 aggregated lesson said exactly this and was never converted into a Builder-side gate, so it recurred at slice-052 — same wording, same missed-by-both-Critic-layers outcome. The Builder-side gate (BC-PROJ-10) closes the structural axis; the Critic-prompt dimension closing the human-judgement axis is a strong `/critic-calibrate` input. (slice-052)
- **The annotated-copy parity contract is *operational*, not byte-invariant** (slice-052 B2 generalized): for any JS↔Python round-trip, the right parity test is consumer-end byte-equality (`backlog.md` == golden) + parsed-payload equality (`parse_html_state` annotations) — NOT a whole-file diff against the browser save. The browser's `document.documentElement.outerHTML` is a full DOM re-serialization the engine normalizes (attribute order, whitespace, entity reencoding); unreproducible from Python *and* irrelevant to the only consumer. Carry into any future browser↔Python parity surfaces. (slice-052)
- **Step-5 in-place substitution inside HTML/XML/template payloads MUST use match-span string slicing, NEVER `re.sub`** (slice-052 M-add-2): Python `re.sub` / `re.Match.expand` treat `\g<…>`, `\1`, and bare backslashes in the *replacement* string specially, corrupting any payload that legitimately contains them (e.g. a JSON `<\/` escape or a user-supplied free-text note with a backslash). Use `text[:m.start(1)] + new_inner + text[m.end(1):]`. The dual-Critic stack stops at the `json.dumps` boundary by default — extend its attention one hop further into where the payload is *written* and re-read. (slice-052)
- **Mechanical-not-honour-system boundary for any slice-level rule deviation** (slice-052 M4): when a slice carves out an exception to a Hard rule (ADR-style deviation), enforcement MUST be helper-side and `Path.resolve()`/grep-test-pinned — never SKILL.md prose telling Claude to self-restrict. A SKILL.md instruction asking Claude to self-restrict IS honour-system. Route the exception through a dedicated helper subcommand with a containment check + a log of refusals. (slice-052)
- **Subprocess child UTF-8 capture is a Windows discipline** (slice-052 build FINDING): any test/harness invoking a UTF-8-reconfigured child from a parent Python on Windows MUST pass `encoding="utf-8"` to `subprocess.run` — the parent's cp1252 default mis-decodes the child's correct UTF-8 emoji bytes. Codify in the test's `run()` helper. Also: a long-running `tools/*.py` entrypoint's `_reconfigure_stdout()` MUST reconfigure BOTH stdout AND stderr — `SystemExit` refusal messages with em-dashes are written to stderr and crash identically. (slice-052)

- **The OSDG-1 guarded-skill member-addition shape is N=2 confirmed clean across skill classes** (slice-049 openers triage/adopt → slice-051 in-loop reflect): verbatim test clone reusing `assert_md_forward_synced` + 4-part PMI-1 bump + ADR-extends-lineage (`supersedes: null`) + content-bearing v-entry-pin + CLAUDE.md enumeration + one pipe-free shippability row mirroring the prior row. Reusable verbatim for any future guarded-`.md` member-addition; the openers-vs-in-loop distinction is immaterial (OSDG-1's "Opener-Skill" name is now an explicitly-decoupled historical label, ADR-053). (slice-051)
- **A mid-slice genuine-contrast perturbation of a git-tracked file MUST restore via saved-temp-bytes / inverse-edit + a pre/post content-hash assertion from the START — NEVER `git checkout -- <path>` / `git restore` / `git stash`** (trips Critical BC-PROJ-3/BC-GLOBAL-2 even when provably harmless; under BRANCH-1 a git-level revert of a slice-touched path destroys uncommitted slice work). The dual-Critic stack does not flag the restore-mechanism hazard implied by an ACCEPTED perturbation plan; the build-time BC-1 gate is the structural backstop (slice-037 law — do NOT add a Critic dimension). (slice-051)
- **A content-bearing v-entry-pin defeats the slice-037 M-add-1 tautological-green class for a drift-guard's primary deliverable ONLY in combination with the prose-pin enumeration + the collected test** — budget all three surfaces at /design-slice for any drift-guard member-addition; the equality test alone is green for stale-but-equal. (slice-051)

- **A "leg N has no deterministic gate" gap is retired cheapest by a verbatim structural clone of the proven sibling gate** + a CSP-1 comparator-parity pin (AVFS-1 = MCFS-1 with two path constants swapped + retargeted docstring/`_ATTRIB`); the standalone-vs-fold choice is an ADR sub-decision (lowest-blast-radius = standalone, sibling untouched). (slice-050)
- **For any new-`tools/*.py` slice the PMI-1 "4-part bump" is really a ~6-surface inventory fan-out** — VERSION + plugin.yaml.version + plugin.yaml tool-path + install_audit._CANONICAL_TOOLS + test_utf8 _ROOT_ONLY_TOOLS + **INSTALL.md hard-coded "N executable methodology tools" count (×2)**. The INSTALL.md count is the dual-Critic-stack blind surface, reachable only by the full-methodology-suite pre-finish sweep (BC-PROJ-4 backstop, not the Critic). Now codified as **BC-PROJ-9**. (slice-050)
- **A first-Critic "recompute the mechanism vs the cited precedent analogy" catch can overturn a design's load-bearing rationale entirely** — slice-050 M3: `essential-unregistered`-by-MCFS-1-analogy was false (`_ESSENTIAL_SHAPES` is changelog-path-only); real ground is environment-mutable-state (slice-029/030A). Budget the enforcing-artifact grep at /design-slice authoring, not /critique. Builder false-precedent guard reconfirmed N+1 (slice-048/049). (slice-050)
- **The slice that closes leg N must check whether it opens an analogous unguarded leg N+1** (DR-1 meta-Critic M-add-1) — closing `ai-sdlc-VERSION` opened an unguarded `reflect/SKILL.md` drift surface; record it as a Discovered nomination, never call "must-not-defer + self-run" *sufficient* when the slice's own R-7 argument says otherwise. (slice-050)
- **A drift-guard family member-addition whose slice has no other bump reason IS a methodology-surface behavior change** (ADR-051 / Critic-B2 resolution) — resolves the ambiguity slice-049 rev-0 got wrong; future member-additions take the `## vN` + 4-part-PMI-1 bump path, NOT the slice-019/021-rode-an-existing-bump non-precedent. (slice-049)
- **Builder recompute-don't-trust must extend to one's OWN /critique ACCEPTED-FIXED edits, especially test re-citations**: slice-049's rev-1 B1-fix re-cited a real PMI-1 test to the WRONG file; caught only by the TPHD-1 /build-slice pre-flight (slice-032 "a design correction is itself an unguarded adversarial surface" class, N+1). Durable cure is the existing TPHD-1 gate, NOT a new Critic dimension (slice-037 law). (slice-049)
- **The 4-part PMI-1 bump's installed `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` leg has no deterministic forward-sync gate** (MCFS-1 covers only the changelog leg); it silently drifted at slice-048 (N=2 with slice-035 DEVIATION-2). Strong next-slice candidate: an MCFS-1-analogue gate for `ai-sdlc-VERSION`. Until then Critic-M2's per-slice pre-sync-diff is the only control. (slice-049)
- **Dual-Critic+DR-1 paid off decisively AGAIN on a methodology-codification slice (N+1 to 026/029/038/039/046/047/048→049)**: first Critic caught the load-bearing false-precedent (the slice's own MEPD-1(b) self-violation, RSAD-1); meta-Critic EXTEND added 2 genuine load-bearing misses with ZERO false positives across both layers. First Critic's recurring blind spot = contract/regression-scope-vs-internal-logic — strong `/critic-calibrate` input. (slice-049)

- **A pure-additive prose-codification slice that injects a new rule into existing artifacts MUST check each host artifact for a pre-existing literal that contradicts the rule** (RSAD-1 surface-consistency — the slice-022 self-violation law generalized from "the codifying slice satisfies the rule" to "the rule does not ship beside its own counter-example on the same artifact"). First Critic blind to this; DR-1 is the structural backstop. N+1 to the standing "DR-1 keeps paying on methodology-codification slices" law (026/029/038/039/046/047→048); per the slice-037 law the cure is DR-1, NOT a new first-Critic dimension/build-check. (slice-048)
- **Builder false-precedent guard, reconfirmed**: never assert in design.md that "slice-N / rule-X retired/decoupled Y" without grepping the enforcing test/artifact in the SAME authoring step. The first Critic's MEPD-1(b) recompute-don't-trust caught it (B1); the durable Builder-side cure is the design-time grep, not relying on the Critic. (slice-048)
- **Genuine-contrast for a pure-additive codification needs ZERO revert ops**: pin the new literal (grep-verified absent pre-edit) + negative guards on the literal being removed (present pre-edit) → one-directional non-tautological FAIL→PASS. Cheaper than slice-045's revert-2-edits and slice-046's positive+negative-on-one-file techniques; reusable for every rule-codification slice. (slice-048)
- Discovered latent gap (N=1, next-slice candidate, not a risk): triage/adopt SKILL.md have no mini-CAD in-repo↔installed drift guard (unlike build_slice/commit_slice/critique/query_design/slice + agents/critique.md CAD-1); their forward-sync is manual-must-not-defer-only. (slice-048)

- **A load-bearing EXTERNAL-PLATFORM behavior (resolution/precedence/quota/API contract) a slice's *premise* depends on must be WebFetch-verified against official docs at /design-slice (or /risk-spike'd) BEFORE design lock — never asserted-from-belief and deferred to /critique. slice-047 burned a full design→critique→critique-review→TRI-1 cycle on a false premise a 10-min doc fetch would have refuted. Now codified as **BC-GLOBAL-3**. (slice-047)
- **Claude Code platform precedence is NOT symmetric across artifact classes**: skills resolve personal>project; subagents resolve project>user (the inverse). Verify each class independently — verify-one-then-generalize is a first-Critic blind shape (caught by DR-1; `/critic-calibrate` watch-list, N=1). (slice-047)
- **A BLOCKED/withdrawn slice can still ship durable value as a decision record** — rewriting its own un-shipped ADR in place (same slice/ID, /build-slice never ran → not SUP-1) converts a dead feature into a recorded decision that closes the deferral that motivated it. (slice-047)

- **On a slice that reclassifies an existing gate's behavior (not a new rule), the first Critic optimizes the mechanical pin/changelog surface and under-weights the NOVEL control-flow edge that is the slice's raison d'être** — slice-046: first Critic filed M1 (negative-anchor literal) + m1 (changelog header shape) precisely but MISSED m-add-1 (unbounded post-auto-invoke re-verification loop on the new `/repro` edge); DR-1 caught it. Budget DR-1 as the structural backstop for the new edge's re-entrancy/termination; durable cure is DR-1, not a new first-Critic dimension (slice-037 law). N+1 to the 026/029/038/039 "DR-1 keeps paying on codification slices" pattern (reclassification sub-class). `/critic-calibrate` watch-list, N=1. (slice-046)
- **Genuine-contrast test-first works cleanly for a prose-reclassification AC without reverting anything**: pin a positive (the new canonical phrase, grep-verified ABSENT pre-edit) + a negative (the exact old literal taken verbatim from the file, grep-verified PRESENT pre-edit). Both flip in one direction when the edit lands — a non-tautological FAIL→PASS, independently verifiable per-literal, cheaper than slice-045's revert-2-edits technique. (slice-046)
- **STP-1 Sub-form A "clean by construction" is the validated pattern for a prose-repoint of a pinned `SKILL.md` section**: preserve every pre-existing positive-pin literal + section anchor verbatim, make the new positive pin's literal the deliverable, keep the new negative pin an `ast.NotIn` node (Sub-form-A-excluded) → zero stale-pin realignment. The "rule minted in slice N is a blind spot on N+1" risk (STP-1 minted slice-044, slice-046 an early governed slice) was pre-empted explicitly in design and held in practice. Also: PCA-1's `pipeline_chain_audit` reads ONLY the 4 required fields + the `**user-input gates**` literal (not gate-list contents) — verified before relying on it, so a user-input-gate-line reword is contract-safe. PCA-1's lighter-checkpoint-beats-hand-off principle extends to safety-rationale gates (replace a mechanical hand-off guarding a real failure mode with a confirm-the-specific-input structured-options checkpoint — protection survives, punt does not). (slice-046)
- **A `**Test-first**: true` mission-brief MUST map EVERY numbered AC to ≥1 TF-1 plan row at `/slice` authoring time.** `tools.test_first_audit --strict-pre-finish` enforces per-AC presence mechanically and does NOT honor a prose "AC-N is grep-only/meta" exemption note — an unmapped AC FAILs at `/build-slice` pre-finish (cost an in-build remediation at slice-045). For a genuinely non-test AC: fold it into a tested AC, give it a real regression test (often a strict improvement — slice-045 AC4 closed the Critic-m2 gap permanently this way), or don't number it as a separate AC. NEW first-Critic-miss class (brief-internal TF-1-plan completeness vs design-soundness; both Critic layers missed it). N=1 — promote to a build-check if it recurs. (slice-045)
- **"In-house methodology surface" (MEPD-1 / mandatory-Critic trigger) is broader than `agents/critique.md:122`'s literal enumeration** (`skills/*/SKILL.md`, `agents/*.md`, `tools/**/*.py`, `methodology-changelog.md`) — `INSTALL.md` (the INST-1 recipe a user executes verbatim) counts. Fail-closed: discharge **MEPD-1(b) by name**, verify why-none against the ACTUAL META-1 `^## v…`-split assertion (`test_methodology_changelog.py:136`) not the precedent alone, and record any no-VERSION-bump risk-retirement on the canonical `risk-register.md` `**Retired**:` surface — NEVER reflection.md (slice-040/R-10 + slice-036/R-9 class). A born-retired entry (discovered+retired same slice) is RR-1-legitimate for a latent defect surfaced+fixed+guarded in one slice. `/critic-calibrate` watch-list N=1: widen the enumeration if it recurs. (slice-045)
- **Test-first purity is cheap and worth it even for descriptive-prose ACs** — reverting 2 prose edits to get a genuine FAIL→PASS on a new AC test cost ~4 trivial ops and produced a real permanent regression guard + non-tautological contrast (the BC-PROJ-5 genuine-contrast discipline applied to a prose AC; slice-045 AC4). (slice-045)
- **The dual-Critic+DR-1 stack is precise on what it reviews (~20 findings ~100% VALIDATED ~0 FALSE-ALARM over 2 rounds × 2 layers) but structurally blind to the audit-vs-real-artifact interaction — the exact law STP-1 codifies, which fired N≥5 inside STP-1's own construction (gitignored-vault, `Or`-disjunction FP, double-count, markdown-pipe shift, cp1252 parity).** The durable cure is build-time real-artifact execution + plan-mode code-reality reads, NOT a new Critic dimension (slice-037 law, N+1…N+5). For any slice shipping a repo-scanning audit/tool, the slice's own first run against the real repo is the decisive validation artifact (slice-038 dogfood law generalized). Strong /critic-calibrate input. (slice-044)
- **Methodology-tooling targeting `architecture/**` must design for live on-disk reads — the entire vault is gitignored (`.gitignore:11`); git-diff/show of vault paths is structurally unavailable.** Verify self-application at /build-slice plan-mode, before building. Now codified as **BC-PROJ-8**. (slice-044)
- **A new `tools/*.py` audit with `main()` carries two off-its-own-logic self-application obligations: (a) add it to `test_utf8_stdout_regression.py`'s argv-classified cp1252 list (verify the argv contract, slice-038), (b) its shippability row must be pipe-free/`\|`-escaped (a raw `|` shifts the 6-col schema → SCMD-1 mis-segment). Sequence both LAST + read the SRSC-1 runner output.** Now codified as **BC-PROJ-7**. (slice-044)
- **`Or` vs `And` prose-pin semantics**: a disjunction (`A in c or B in c`) pins NO operand individually (an absent alternative is intentional — false-positive if per-operand-flagged); a conjunction pins each. Any prose-pin-analysis tooling must distinguish `ast.Or` (exclude) from `ast.And` (per-operand). (slice-044)
- **A risk-retirement edit MUST flip the `**Status**:` field line — RR-1 parses status SOLELY from `**Status**:`, never the `**Retired**:` prose; adding only `**Retired**:` silently false-greens the risk as its old status.** Plus: re-derive the verification command's filter axis from the risk's ACTUAL prior state (`open→retired` ⇒ assert absent from `--filter-status open` + `--json status=="retired"`; NOT `--filter-status mitigating`, vacuous unless the risk passed through mitigating like R-4/R-5). Both reduce to one root: copying a sibling retirement's shape instead of recomputing from this risk's state. Now codified as **BC-PROJ-6**. (slice-043)
- **For a conformance/audit slice, the AC3 genuine-contrast proof is: write the regression test FIRST, run it against the unmodified tool, capture the FAIL, then implement** — the FAIL→PASS transition on the same assertions is the non-tautology evidence, cheaper and more conclusive than reasoning about tautology. (slice-043)
- **Builder recompute-don't-trust on the methodology-obligation why-none (verify vs the REAL META-1 `## v`-split + PMI-1 already-listed-tool invariant, not the slice-029/036/040 precedent) is now a routine plan-mode action** — applied proactively at slice-043 /build-slice, discharged must-not-defer #1 with no late /reflect adjudication. Confirms slice-040 MEPD-1(b). (slice-043)
- **For any identifier/rename/carve-out slice: prove "frozen set untouched" with a regex-INDEPENDENT sha256 tree-hash snapshot (pre/post), never a regex/`grep -c` count** — `grep -c`=lines≠occ; a Python `(?:)` non-capturing group silently fails under ERE `grep` (slice-042 archive baseline 22 vs true 121). Now codified as **BC-PROJ-5**. (slice-042)
- **For inventory-accuracy slices: embed the authoritative anchor command in design.md + declare the build-step-1 grep the single source of truth; never hand-transcribe counts** — rev-0→rev-1→rev-2 took two Critic layers + build-T0 to converge; the structural cure is to stop transcribing. BC-PROJ-5. (slice-042)
- **Recompute-don't-trust now fires on the Builder's OWN /critique ACCEPTED-FIXED fix, N≥4 (slice-032/034/041/042)** — a Critic "count X wrong" is correctly accepted but the *replacement* is transcribed from a non-authoritative grep; DR-1 B-add-1/2/3 is the backstop. Strong /critic-calibrate input. (slice-042)
- **slice-032 "a design correction is itself an unguarded adversarial surface" recurs at ADR-prose level (N+1)** — the M3-sev fix re-introduced the literal it corrected into ADR-045; invisible to Critic+DR-1, caught only by build-T0 repo-wide anchored re-derivation. Candidate Builder-plan-mode checklist item. (slice-042)
- **DR-1 calibration (highest-signal meta-Critic miss to date)**: a critique-review "independently verified mechanically sound" on an AST-classification / audit-parse claim is worthless unless the meta-Critic *instantiated the classifier* against the real (and edit-simulated) artifact — reading the shape constant + the changed line is necessary-but-insufficient. slice-041 rev-1 DR-1 reasoned-not-executed `classify_fn` and false-confirmed, letting a BLOCKED slice's flaw relocate undetected for a full revision. "Execute don't reason" is now N≥3 for the DR-1 layer (slice-032/034/041). Strong `/critic-calibrate` input. (slice-041)
- **State-transition slices** (risk mitigating→retired, ADR accepted→superseded, a gate-verdict flip) MUST pre-grep for pre-existing tests pinning the OLD state value and realign them in the SAME fix block (slice-039 generalized). slice-041's R-4 retirement FAILed `test_r_4_..._stays_mitigating`; neither the design nor 3 Critic revisions / 3 DR-1 passes enumerated it — only the pre-finish BC-PROJ-4 full-suite run caught it. Candidate Builder-plan-mode checklist item. (slice-041)
- **Split→one-pass convergence, 3rd confirmation** (slice-030A, 031, 041): when 2 BLOCKED loops show the flaw *relocating* each revision, a structural pivot beats a 3rd patch — the relocation surface is removed, not moved. Don't wait for the meta-Critic to force it; self-check after BLOCKED #1. (slice-041)
- **A negative-invariant pin must target the executable surface, not raw text** — `"X not in catalog_text"` is wrong when X legitimately appears in prose (slice-041's relocation-proof over-tightened to whole-catalog vs the Machine-cmd column; self-caught at pre-finish). (slice-041)
- **A discipline rule minted in slice N is a first-Critic blind spot on slice N+1 (the first slice it governs); the DR-1 meta-Critic is the structural backstop.** slice-040: MEPD-1 (minted slice-039) governs exactly slice-040's no-changelog-entry decision, yet neither the first Critic nor the Builder named it — only DR-1 M-add-1 did (substance was already MEPD-1(b)-compliant). When a slice's central decision class is governed by a rule minted in the immediately-prior slice, explicitly task the first Critic to check that rule by name. Strong `/critic-calibrate` watch-list candidate. (slice-040)
- **For prose-pin-class slices, make the `/reflect` Step 5.3 shippability critical-path entry the realigned/added mini-CAD pin itself** — an uncatalogued mini-CAD pin's breakage is invisible to the catalog runner (the exact slice-038→R-10 ~5-slice detection-latency class). slice-040 catalogued the realigned pin as #40, retro-closing the gap. (slice-040)
- **A no-VERSION-bump conformance-fix risk-retirement is recorded in `risk-register.md` `**Retired**:` line (+ ADR only if a code-behavior decision is locked) — NEVER a parentless methodology-changelog `###` entry (META-1 splits on `## v`).** slice-036/R-9 + slice-040/R-10 are the two precedents; verify why-none against the actual META-1 assertion, not the precedent alone (MEPD-1(b) / slice-032 false-precedent guard). (slice-040)

- **A Critic-proposed test oracle (count / `not in X` membership / expected-value set) is a CLAIM about the artifact — recompute it mechanically BEFORE applying it as ACCEPTED-FIXED.** slice-039: first-Critic m2 prescribed `"_lists_eleven_sub_clauses" not in catalog` + count 14; reality = 15 occurrences and that pin would GUARANTEED-false-FAIL on shippability line-34's frozen slice-025 narrative. DR-1 M-add-1 re-opened the already-"fixed" finding. slice-032/036 "reason about the claim, not the artifact" law → now extended to Critic-proposed oracles applied at TRI-1. (slice-039)
- **Frozen-history occurrences of a renamed anchor live on MULTIPLE surfaces; FBCD-1 sub-mode (a) full-fileset grep must enumerate ALL twins — especially on the surface the slice edits.** slice-039: first-Critic enumerated the changelog:266 twin but not the shippability line-34 twin where SCPD-1 actually renames. - **A SKILL.md-repointing slice must supersede/realign any `test_*_skill.py` mini-CAD prose-pin asserting the OLD prose in the SAME fix block** — SCPD-1/mini-CAD propagation applied to prose-pins, not just shippability rows. slice-038 SRSC-1 repointed /validate-slice Step 5.5 but left the slice-031 prose-pin stale → R-10 (methodology suite non-green on master since slice-038, undetected until slice-039's BC-PROJ-4 full-suite pre-finish run). Strongest standing follow-up candidate. (slice-039)
- **DR-1 dual-review keeps paying decisively on codification slices (N+1: 026/029/038/039)** — M-add-1 prevented a guaranteed downstream false-FAIL on an already-ACCEPTED-FIXED finding; structural blind spots stay backstopped by BC-PROJ-4 + real-artifact runs, not new Critic dimensions. (slice-039)
- **A Critic/meta-Critic prescription "add tool X to parametrized list L" is a CLAIM about L's argv contract, not a verified fact — even when the meta-Critic states it "verified" the bucket.** slice-038: both Critic layers blessed `_POSITIONAL_SLICE_TOOLS` (passes a slice-folder arg) for a catalog-file-path tool; the meta-Critic explicitly "verified correct bucket" by reasoning about `_positional_slice_argv` shape without checking the sibling precedent or running the argv.
