(the two shippability siblings are deliberately NOT — they use bespoke tests). slice-032 reason-about-the-claim law N+1; slice-034 "task the Critic to empirically EXECUTE the proposed thing" would have caught it; build-time artifact-read is the structural backstop (no new Critic dimension per slice-037). (slice-038)
- **For a tool sharing a private helper across modules, assert object-identity reuse in a test (`consumer._fn is source._fn`)** — converts "reuses, not re-derives" from prose into a mechanically-enforced CSP-1 contract. Cheap, high-signal; recommend for any cross-`tools` private-reuse slice. (slice-038)
- **The "run the shipped tool on the full real artifact at the slice's own /validate-slice" dogfood is the decisive validation artifact for runner/executor-class slices** — same role BC-PROJ-4 plays for audit-parse slices. slice-038 SRSC-1: the new runner running the full real 38-row catalog 38/38 proved the R-8 fix on reality more conclusively than any unit fixture. Keep it the mid-slice + validate gate for this class. (slice-038)
- **DR-1 reliably recalibrates first-Critic single-precedent→Blocker inflation.** slice-038 B2: first-Critic filed Blocker on "every prior RULE-ID slice has the paired test" (actually N=1, only PTFFD-1); Builder caught the evidence error, DR-1 correctly reclassified Blocker→Major. The dual layer is doing its job on severity, not just findings — `/critic-calibrate` watch-list note if the inflation pattern recurs. (slice-038)
- **Audit-codification slices self-apply recursively at THREE layers — TF-1-plan (B1), shippability-catalog-row resolution under the new audit (M-add-2), and catalog-row incidental-coupling to gitignored state (SCMD-1, pre-finish-only). Layers 2 & 3 are reachable ONLY by the pre-finish real-artifact runs, never the Critic stack; sequence the new audit's own catalog row LAST.** (slice-037)
- **Plan-mode `ls`/stat of every cited new test directory must be the FIRST plan-mode action** — the dual-Critic stack incl. meta-Critic reads path strings but does not verify directory existence; `tests/tools/`→`tests/methodology/` phantom-path-convention missed by 4 gates, caught in one plan-mode read. N≥2 (slice-027-B1, slice-037). (slice-037)
- **A `-D`-rule refinement mints a NEW `-D` ID, never a `vN.N` label** (PTFFD-1←PTFCD-1; the `vN.N` label is exclusively the NON-`-D` audit-gate naming class; TFFL-1↔TF-1 governing precedent; ADR-038 codifies it for the `-D` family). (slice-037)
- **A content-bearing AC needs a CONTENT pin, not a byte-equality/forward-sync pin** — a CAD-1/drift-equality test passes whether or not the content change exists (tautological green for any AC whose deliverable is prose/a rule/a sub-clause); use the slice-025 `test_critique_dim_9_*` content-pin precedent. (slice-037, meta-Critic M-add-1)
- **Dual-Critic stack hit 12/12 VALIDATED / 0 FALSE-ALARM again on a codification slice; precision is not the problem.** Its two standing blind spots (audit-self-violation reachable only by real-artifact runs; phantom-path reachable only by plan-mode stat) are structural — the existing backstops (plan-mode + SCMD-1/BC-PROJ-4 pre-finish) cover them; do NOT add Critic dimensions for these. (slice-037)
- **The dual-Critic stack files with high precision but still misses its two standing blind-spot classes (audit-self-violation; RULE-ID/entry-pin obligation); BC-PROJ-4 (run the changed audit on the real artifact at pre-finish), not the Critic stack, is the structural backstop.** slice-036: TF-1 `--strict-pre-finish` refused a Critic-blessed "AC3 carve-out survives strict TF-1" claim (slice-022 self-violation N+1). Never trust a Critic-blessed "survives strict <AUDIT>" claim without running the audit on the real artifact. (slice-036)
- **A Critic-proposed test oracle is a claim about the artifact, not the artifact — recompute fixture-derived expected values (scores/bands) before encoding them.** slice-036: M2/M-add-1 proposed `--filter-band high == {R1,R3}`; R3 is score-3 medium band; plan-mode reading the fixture + `_band_for_score` caught it. Generalizes slice-032 "Critic reasons about the claim not the artifact" to Critic-proposed expected-values. (slice-036)
- **Methodology-surface *behavior* changes (not just new rules) carry a changelog/RULE-ID/PMI-1 obligation question the Critic stack does not raise — pre-decide it at /design-slice, verified against the enforcing audit (slice-029 discipline), never auto-blessed "no entry" (slice-032 DEVIATION-1 anti-pattern) nor adjudicated late at /reflect.** slice-036 resolved it post-hoc: empirically not gate-required (PMI-1/changelog suite clean; ADR + risk-retirement sufficient for a conformance fix). Confirms 2026-05-17 /critic-calibrate Proposal 2 — the proposals must actually be applied to agents/critique.md. (slice-036)
- **`/repro` before the slice branch exists leaves the slice's own first artifact uncommitted on the default branch — `git checkout -b` correctly carries it onto the slice branch; not a BRANCH-1 contamination case (confirm with user, don't stash slice-owned WIP).** (slice-036)
- **An identifier rename has two non-interchangeable reference classes; a prose string-replace silently ships broken executable binds.** slice-035: executable filesystem-path binds (`(ROOT/"skills"/"<name>"/…).read_text()`, iterated path-list constants, test-module filenames + `test_<name>_*` fn defs) hard-fail the suite on a directory rename; prose `/<name>` refs don't. Enumerate them in separate buckets, run a pre- AND post-edit inventory grep, and treat a surviving bind as a Bucket-A-class failure. The real-artifact post-edit grep — not Critic reasoning — is the structural backstop (rev-1 of the inventory-completion slice itself shipped an incomplete inventory; 14 dual-Critic findings corrected it — slice-022 self-violation law). Codified as SRCD-1. (slice-035)
- **The installed-side configuration set is the first Critic's recurring structural blind spot on version-bumping methodology slices.** slice-035 B-add-1 (load-bearing, ONLY caught by DR-1 meta-Critic): the PMI-1 atomic bump is 4-part (`VERSION` + `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + forward-synced `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md`) — every prior changelog entry documents this, yet the first Critic + the design both said "triple", dropping the installed `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` leg (slice-006 DEVIATION-2 / slice-007-B1 recurrence, now N≥3). Strong `/critic-calibrate` input: a first-Critic Dim-7/Dim-9 sub-bullet that, for any version-bumping methodology slice, enumerates all four atomic-bump legs incl. the installed VERSION file. Also: sequence installed-copy reconciliation BEFORE any INST-1 gate (it audits the installed tree). (slice-035)
- **Tasking the first Critic to EMPIRICALLY EXECUTE a changed regex/parser against adversarial inputs reaches the audit-vs-artifact defect class previously believed Critic-unreachable.** slice-034 M1: the first Critic ran the proposed `(true|false)\b.*$` against `false-positive` and caught a narrower R-7 self-violation AT /critique (design-time) — directly counter to slice-031/033's recorded "the dual-Critic stack structurally cannot reach the audit-parsing-the-artifact interaction; only BC-PROJ-4 can." For any slice changing an audit's own parse rule, the /critique prompt must explicitly demand empirical pattern-testing (not reasoning). Strongest `/critic-calibrate` input since the FBCD-1 cluster — candidate Dim-9 surgical sub-bullet. (slice-034)
- **Methodology-surface behavior-change slices need a Critic checklist pass for the RULE-ID + `test_v_0_NN_0_*` entry-pin obligation, separate from the deep dive.** slice-034: the first Critic verified PMI-1 version *mechanics* but missed that a behavior-changing changelog entry needs a *minted RULE-ID + entry-pin* (the slice-032 DEVIATION-1 class); the DR-1 meta-Critic caught it (M-add-1). "Deep-dive crowds out the mechanically-derivable checklist item" recurs — candidate Dim-7 sub-bullet at the next `/critic-calibrate`. (slice-034)
- **BC-PROJ-4 dogfood remains the single most decisive validation artifact for audit-parse-rule slices** — running the changed audit on the slice's OWN brief ("ENABLED N row(s)" vs the pre-fix "not enabled") proves the fix on the real artifact more conclusively than any unit fixture; keep it the mid-slice + pre-finish gate for this class. (slice-034)
- **slice-022 self-violation law ~N≈10 — a methodology/audit-tooling slice will commit the exact defect class its own risk register documents; the BC-PROJ-4 real-artifact pre-finish gate run (not the Critic stack) is the structural backstop.** slice-033 nearly shipped R-7 (its own open risk): `/slice`'s idiomatic `**Test-first**: true  (per TF-1 — …)` mission-brief annotation broke `test_first_audit.py`'s `\s*$`-anchored regex → TF-1 silent default-off on a genuinely test-first slice. BOTH the first-Critic AND the DR-1 meta-Critic missed it (despite R-7 being OPEN and the meta-Critic being asked about TF-1 vocabulary) — the dual-Critic stack structurally cannot reach the audit-parsing-the-artifact interaction. Caught only by BC-PROJ-4 (run every affected gate on the REAL artifact at pre-finish + read its output: "not enabled" on a test-first slice = silent-bypass alarm). For any audit/gate-touching slice: budget the BC-PROJ-4 real-artifact run as the PRIMARY R-6/R-7-class catch. (slice-033)
- **Declaration-check vs state-check is a recurring high-value Critic catch on infra/config slices.** slice-033 M1: `.gitattributes eol=lf` + `git add --renormalize` updates the INDEX only — the already-CRLF working tree needs an explicit re-checkout to materialize LF. The Critic-mandated working-tree-STATE test (`no \r\n in guarded files`) caught what a `git check-attr` declaration check would have shipped green. When a fix verifies a declaration (attribute, config key) rather than the resulting state, demand the state assertion. (slice-033)
- **A naive shippability Step-5.5 runner that strips only OUTER backticks (not per-`;`-segment) false-FAILs the lone multi-segment catalog row** — slice-032 secondary discovery, reproduced LIVE at slice-033 (row #28 WinError 2 under outer-strip; PASSES under SCMD-1-correct per-segment parse). The runner contract is unpinned → **R-8** opened. A future runner-hardening slice should pin per-`;`-segment backtick-strip via audit + `skills/validate-slice/SKILL.md` prose. (slice-033)
- **The dual-Critic stack does NOT verify prior-slice precedent claims — it reasons about the claim, not the artifact.** slice-032: a Builder disposition (m1) cited a "slice-029 no-rule-lineage precedent" to omit a bespoke changelog entry-pin; the first Critic filed it Minor and the meta-Critic *explicitly confirmed* it — yet the precedent was factually false (24/24 versions pinned, slice-029 included). Caught only at `/build-slice` plan-mode by reading the actual `test_methodology_changelog.py`. The slice-029 "pre-read the enforcing audit's actual assertion" lesson now generalizes from Builder to Critic. When a design/disposition cites "slice-NNN did/didn't X", the Critic MUST open the artifact. Strong `/critic-calibrate` input (high-leverage: a false precedent survived 2 adversarial passes + dual-review). (slice-032)
- **A design correction discovered at build-plan-mode is itself an unguarded adversarial surface — route it through the FULL re-critique loop.** slice-032 DEVIATION-1: the correction committed the exact slice-022 self-violation it cited (v2 M1: "mirrors scmd_1" specified 2 of scmd_1's 4 assertions) AND the fix re-introduced the single-site-coverage silent-drift class at N=2 (dual-review-v2 M-add-v2-1). Corrections are not trusted edits; the full `/critique`+`/critique-review` loop on the delta paid off twice. (slice-032)
- **`/build-slice` plan-mode is a load-bearing backstop for false-precedent claims the dual-Critic stack rubber-stamps.** slice-032: the "design is wrong" gate caught what 2 Critic passes + dual-review missed. Treat plan-mode artifact-reading as a first-class verification layer, not a formality. (slice-032)
- **A shippability-catalog runner that does not implement SCMD-1's deterministic `;`-split produces false-FAILs on multi-segment Machine-cmd rows.** slice-032: row #28 false-FAILed under an ad-hoc runner; both segments PASS under correct `;`-split. The `fix-skill-drift-test-crlf-normalization` (R-5, now N+2) candidate should also pin the Step-5.5 runner `;`-split contract. (slice-032)
- **Methodology-tooling slices: the audit's OWN parse rules are a design-stage blind spot (N+3).** slice-031: a trailing annotation on `**Test-first**: true` silently disabled the entire TF-1 gate via default-off semantics (R-7); a letter-suffixed slice folder broke BRANCH-1's numeric `slice-NNN-` regex (R-6). Critics review intent, not the audit-vs-artifact interaction. Mitigation: at /build-slice prerequisite AND pre-finish, run every affected gate on the *actual artifact* (not unit fixtures) and read its output — "not enabled"/"usage-error"/"0 rows" on a slice that should engage the gate is a silent-bypass alarm. Promoted to **BC-PROJ-4**. (slice-031)
- **AST/text-classification audits need a real-corpus mid-slice smoke gate at ~50% — design-stage reasoning cannot enumerate the false-positive surface.** slice-031: the SCMD-1 classifier needed 3 corrections (blanket-Path.home over-reach on 39 unrelated fns; synthetic `tmp_path` BCI-1 trees false-flagged; const-aliased corpus root unresolved), each caught ONLY by the smoke gate vs the real catalog, none by 4 Critic/meta passes. slice-030A's "smoke gate = empirical fidelity oracle" now N+2. (slice-031)
- **Split → one-pass convergence, confirmed a 2nd time.** slice-031: v1 BLOCKED → TRI-1 b-split → v2 NEEDS-FIXES → CLEAN → shipped in ONE build with zero scope/design change, zero flaw-relocation. When the flaw is "this is actually two features," split beats patch — the relocation surface is removed, not relocated (slice-030 was the 1st confirmation). (slice-031)
- **When a slice's canonical id must differ from its split-label (numeric folder vs `NNNx` prose), record the alias up front.** slice-031: `/slice` chose folder `slice-030B-`; BRANCH-1's numeric-NNN regex rejected it → mid-build folder/branch/id rename churn. Decide "numeric folder, letter label in prose only" at `/slice` and write the milestone identity-note then. Next split slice (030C) must use a numeric folder from the start. (slice-031)
- **2 consecutive BLOCKED dual-Critic loops with all-ACCEPTED drafts IS the rubber-stamp / non-convergence signal — stop patching, surface a split/scope-cut decision; don't wait for the meta-Critic to force it.** slice-030A: the meta-Critic's split recommendation converged in ONE pass after 2 failed patch loops where the flaw relocated each time. Self-check after BLOCKED #1. (slice-030A)
- **Never overwrite a prior round's `critique.md`/`critique-review.md` in a re-critique loop — suffix `-vN`.** slice-030A overwrote the v1 triage_audit-clean table writing the re-critique → the v2 meta-Critic could only score M1 SUSPICIOUS because the evidence was deleted (restored as `critique-history-v1.md`). The prior triage table is a load-bearing audit artifact. (slice-030A)
- **Shippability rows/commands must not depend on environment-mutable state — gitignored content (R-4) AND Windows line-ending checkout (R-5).** slice-030A: `test_diagnose_skill_drift` raw-byte in-repo↔installed compare false-FAILed under `core.autocrlf=true` (content byte-identical). 030B's machine-stable command column + drift-test CRLF normalization are the two concrete fixes. (slice-030A)
- **For a non-deterministic step (LLM prose, no function), don't fix a non-existent source — enforce the invariant with a deterministic downstream gate.** slice-030A/ADR-029: `/reflect` Step 5b has no promotion function; BCI-1 (regression-tested) is the durable control. The mid-slice smoke gate is the empirical fidelity oracle when byte-exact recovery is unattainable. (slice-030A)
- **Splitting an over-scoped slice genuinely removes the flaw-relocation surface.** slice-030→030A/030B converged in one critique pass after 2 patch-loop failures. Prefer split over a 3rd patch when the flaw relocates rather than shrinks. (slice-030A)
- **Shippability-catalog rows must not depend on gitignored / never-tracked vault content.** slice-029: rows #5/#8/#12 cite `test_build_checks_audit.py` which reads the gitignored local `architecture/build-checks.md`; when that local file drifted (truncated to 1 rule), the catalog FAILed for environment reasons unrelated to the slice under validation — a false PCA-1 HALT that also masks real regressions. The R-4 follow-up should harden these rows AND give the catalog a machine-stable command column (also kills the ad-hoc-runner Runtime-cell-as-command footgun, #28/#29). (slice-029)
- **Suspected `/reflect` Step 5b append-vs-overwrite defect (R-4).** Both `architecture/build-checks.md` + `~/.claude/build-checks.md` were found truncated to ONLY the most-recently-promoted rule (BC-PROJ-3 / BC-GLOBAL-2), losing BC-PROJ-1/2 + BC-GLOBAL-1. Signature points at the Step-5b promotion path; the R-4 fix slice must inspect it directly (regression test: promote rule #2, assert #1 survives). Failing repro `tests/methodology/test_build_checks_audit.py` already exists → BFRD-1 prelude pre-satisfied. (slice-029)
- **On methodology-surface codification slices, explicitly task the meta-Critic with re-auditing the *applied* fixes** (fresh-inconsistency / slice-022 self-violation lens), not only re-reviewing the first Critic's original findings. slice-029: first-Critic "all-8-ACCEPTED-FIXED" was the predicted too-easy signature; the meta-Critic's self-violation priming pinpointed M-add-2 (ADR-027 still said "retire" inside M1's own fix artifact). slice-022 self-violation law now **N≈9**. (slice-029)
- **Empirical real-shell validation beats asserted portability.** slice-029: the meta-Critic's "unverified MINGW bash-array" concern (M-add-1) was discharged by running the exact 3-arm flag-strip snippet under the real Git-Bash 5.2.37 MINGW64 (7/7 invocations correct), not by argument. Prose/codification slices with an executable artifact should validate the artifact's *runtime behavior*, not just its prose-pins. (slice-029)
- **Pre-read the enforcing audit's actual assertion before committing to a TRI-1-decided artifact shape.** slice-029 plan-mode read `test_each_changelog_entry_carries_rule_reference` and confirmed it needs only the literal `Rule reference` line (not a minted rule-ID) — that de-risked the M2-option-B rule-ID-less `### Changed` entry before any edit, avoiding a late build failure. (slice-029)
- **Destructive `git checkout`/`restore`/`stash` on uncommitted slice work is a class hazard under branch-per-slice** — slice-028's validation harness `git checkout -- <test file>` silently reverted the ENTIRE uncommitted slice refactor to pre-slice HEAD; post-revert green was the OLD sentinel (false-green nearly masked total work loss). Mutate-then-revert MUST use in-place reverse edits / temp copies / monkeypatch + a pre/post hash assertion, never git-level revert. Promoted BC-PROJ-3 + BC-GLOBAL-2. (slice-028)
- **slice-022 self-violation law now N≈8 — DEEPEST yet on an audit-adjacent codification slice.** slice-028 committed its discipline's defect class at design-time (option-3 declaration-weakening B2) AND at fix-design-time (option-4 helper-extraction fail-OPEN B-add-1), each caught one Critic layer deeper, all pre-build. For 3-part-template refactor slices the part-INTERACTION (not per-part correctness) is the meta-Critic's highest-yield target. (slice-028)
- **Self-written AST meta-tests false-positive on their own defensive code** — slice-028's int-Compare shape check caught the helper's `len(args) >= 2` arity guard; the counter-anchor caught the guard tests' own introspection `ast.parse`. slice-014 fidelity = defect-class-precise scoping, not blanket structural bans. Budget one debug iteration for meta-test self-false-positives on any AST-shape-pin slice. (slice-028)

Future codification slices need not gate on a calibration run when cumulative evidence is well past N=2; ADR documents the convention extension. (slice-024)

- **BRANCH-1 shipped at slice-021 (methodology v0.35.0) — first audit-from-day-one codification (vs prior -D-suffix prose-only family).** Branch-per-slice workflow at `/build-slice` `## Prerequisite check ### Branch state` sub-section + `/commit-slice --merge` (LOCAL-ONLY v1; slice-022 redesigns) + `tools/branch_workflow_audit.py` (programmatic gate from day one). (slice-021)

- **NEW Dim 9 sub-class candidate at N=1 (slice-014)**: **`pytest-namespace-package-import-mode-defeats-dotted-string-monkeypatch-target`**. Pytest's default import-mode places test modules in sys.modules under bare keys (e.g., `methodology.test_methodology_changelog`) NOT dotted paths (`tests.methodology.test_methodology_changelog`) when `tests/` is a namespace package without `__init__.py`. Code using dotted-string module-target patterns (`monkeypatch.setattr("dotted.path.attr", val)`, `importlib.import_module("dotted.path")`) imports a separate copy under the dotted key, leaving the running test's namespace untouched. **Same root-cause family as VAL-1 Layer B `tests` namespace-package class (N=12 cumulative recurrence at slice-014)**. Fix: object-form `monkeypatch.setattr(sys.modules[__name__], "REPO_ROOT", tmp_path)`. Promote to Dim 9 sub-class refinement at N=2 if recurs at slice-015+. Joins watch-list for /critic-calibrate next-trigger. (slice-014 — N=1)
